Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors: 1. Process execution (grabber_lab.exe from PowerShell, no GUI). 2. File system operations (Login Data read, file collection, zip creation, cleanup). 3. Network exfiltration via FTP. 4. No persistence. 5. Anti-forensics (zip/tmp deletion). 6. Timeline of 5 key events. 7. IOC (process, file paths, FTP). 8. Checklist compliance. Defects: 1. Missing hash in golden chunk 0. 2. Contradiction in registry activity (student claims RegOpenKey/RegQueryValue but golden has no registry writes). 3. Placeholder in golden chunk 0 (evidence-ID numbering). 4. Material false claim in golden chunk 10 (inferred behavior not supported by evidence). 5. Unresolved evidence reference in golden chunk 11 (evidence links to appendix not provided). 6. Coarse timeline in golden chunk 3 (events not ordered chronologically). 7. Privilege misread in golden chunk 5 (process exit time misattributed). 8. System/tool noise attributed to malware in golden chunk 1 (baseline tool activity mistaken for malware).

| File system | grabber_lab.exe | 1. Đọc C:\Users\labuser\AppData\Local\Google\Chrome\User Data\Default\Login Data và copy sang C:\Users\labuser\AppData\Local\Temp\~ld01.tmp.<br>2. Quét Desktop/Documents theo *.txt, *.docx, *.kdbx và gom vào C:\Users\labuser\AppData\Local\Temp\data_DESKTOP-G1YL305.zip.<br>3. Sau upload xóa zip và ~ld01.tmp để xóa dấu vết. | E03, E04, E06 |
| Registry / config | grabber_lab.exe | Chỉ có thao tác đọc (RegOpenKey/RegQueryValue) khi nạp DLL; không có RegSetValue. | - |
